# Agents · P · part 17

1,135 entries, planner to planner-critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **planner**: Planning role for orchestration mode (see the `orchestrate` skill). Produces the implementation plan (`plan.md`) for a task before any code is written. Defaults to Opus at high effort; the orchestrator picks the tier per spawn by passing `model` and `effort`. Use only when running orchestration mode. ([ijat/orch](https://github.com/ijat/orch/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/planner.md))

- **planner**: Strategic planning consult from a stronger model. Use BEFORE committing to a plan - before entering plan mode on a big task, while drafting a multi-step plan, at mid-execution checkpoints ("we are at step 3 of 7, does the rest still hold?"), or whenever the session wants a more capable model's input on approach, decomposition, or sequencing. Give it the goal, the constraints, and the draft plan (or ask it to shape one); it returns a plan verdict with ordering fixes, missing prerequisites, risks, and a premortem. Read-only - it critiques and shapes plans, it never implements. ([InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra](https://github.com/InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra/blob/HEAD/agents/planner.md))

([rafaelsmc1987/kimiswarm](https://github.com/rafaelsmc1987/kimiswarm/blob/HEAD/plugins/kdr-x/agents/planner-council.md))
- **planner-counterpart**: 수신자 역산형 기획자. 제안 메일을 하루 수십 통 받아 지우던 쪽에 있다가 넘어온 사람. "답장이 나오는 조건"에서 거꾸로 타겟과 메일을 설계한다. plan-debate 스킬의 정반합 토론에 참여하는 세 기획자 중 하나이며, 메일 문안·톤·반응 설계가 필요할 때 단독으로도 쓴다. ([lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan](https://github.com/lgosoup/keunsamchon-agent-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/planner-counterpart.md))
- **planner-critic**: Critique a plan.json against schema, granularity limits, and dev-lessons. Read-only. ([garveyhu/awesome-skills](https://github.com/garveyhu/awesome-skills/blob/HEAD/meta/self-improving-workflow/agents/planner-critic.md) · ★11)
- **planner-critic**: Critical Analyst for the SDLC planner team. Reviews implementation plans for FR/TR/AC alignment, industry best practices, cost compliance, and cross-story integration. Max 2 refinement cycles before proceeding with documented risks. ([sagerstack/multimodal-agentic-expense-claim-kit](https://github.com/sagerstack/multimodal-agentic-expense-claim-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/planner-critic.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
